In practice, induced skills may transfer unreliably and can even harm the agent that retrieves them. When agent-induced skills transfer reliably across tasks remains an open question. We conduct a comprehensive and controlled study of how the way skills are induced shapes their transfer across tasks. Specifically, we compare task-level with subtask-level skill induction and text with code skill formats, the two axes along which existing methods differ. Task-level skills mostly reduce the agent's performance below its no-memory baseline while subtask-level skills raise it above on average, and text skills transfer better than code skills. To further understand our findings, we examine two complementary properties of the induced skills: specificity, which measures how closely a skill matches real tasks, and abstractness, which measures how evenly its relevance spreads across tasks. Neither property alone predicts task success, but their combined effect does, which we propose as a skill utility score. The score correlates consistently with task success when skills are transferred, and subtask-level and text skills score higher. Computing skill utility only needs the skills and task descriptions but not any task execution, so our score serves as a practical diagnostic of a skill memory before any new task runs.

</details>


### [173] [Phantom Gains: Auditing Self-Improvement Against a Measured Null](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20290)
*Cheng Xu,Nan Yan,Liming Chen,M-Tahar Kechadi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出，评估语言模型自训练是否带来真实逐题能力变化时，常见的转移级指标容易被噪声与测量伪影误导；作者提出用冻结控制组估计每个统计量的零假设，并用逐题精确检验和FDR控制替代不稳健的阈值修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前语言模型改进常被按“哪些题目获得/丢失能力”来判断，而非仅看平均准确率。但这种逐题转移分析本质上是在比较两个有噪声的估计，容易产生伪阳性和结论反转。作者希望审计自训练评估中隐藏的测量失败。

Method: 作者在Qwen3-8B上审计三轮rank-32 LoRA自训练，并设置经过相同流程但不训练的冻结控制组；比较多种转移统计与阈值修正方法，识别测量失败；进一步提出基于冻结基线重复实验的逐题精确检验、合并基线估计和错误发现率控制，并在匹配数据流、训练量和评估设置的多臂实验中应用。

Result: 作者发现七类测量失败，且在缺少控制组时会反转报告结论。单次贪婪解码会在未训练模型上制造虚假的能力变化，扩展统计量甚至给冻结模型分配0.280的“获得率”。常见阈值修正在冻结比较中仍有非零零假设。新方法在所有保留复现实验上未检测到显著自训练收益，且对多重检验规则、错误率和池大小不敏感。外部蒸馏能改善基模型较少达到的问题，而三种自训练不能；回归分析表明这种差异不是由蒸馏总体收益更大导致的。

Conclusion: 转移级审计必须为每个报告的统计量单独测量零分布，而不能依赖未经校准的逐题变化或简单阈值修正。冻结基线重复实验可提供无需新增实验成本的零假设估计，但常见研究拥有的重复数量可能不足。

Abstract: Whether a language model has improved itself is increasingly judged not by mean accuracy but by which individual problems it gains and loses. Tracking these transitions means differencing two noisy estimates, leaving them vulnerable to measurement artifacts. Auditing three rounds of rank-$32$ LoRA self-training on Qwen3-8B against a frozen control pushed through the identical pipeline, we identify seven measurement failures, each of which inverts a reported finding when its control is absent. Several are standard practice. A ledger built on a single greedy decode manufactures capability changes on an untrained model, largely an artifact of inference batching; the expansion statistic separating acquisition from sharpening assigns that same model a rate of $0.280$. The natural threshold repair does not survive replication: estimated across the frozen comparisons such a design already contains, its null stays non-zero. We replace it with a per-problem exact test against a pooled baseline under false-discovery-rate control, which detects nothing on any held-out replicate and is unchanged under the multiple-testing rule, error rate and pool size. Applied to a ladder of arms matched in stream, volume and evaluation, the audit finds that external distillation improves problems the base model rarely reaches while three forms of self-training do not; a regression rejects this asymmetry as a by-product of distillation's larger overall gain ($p < 10^{-8}$). On the far smaller set of problems the base model never reaches, the evidence is inconclusive, while self-training corrupts problems solved at baseline at rates well above the measured floor. Transition-level auditing therefore requires a separately measured null for every statistic it reports: nulls that cost no new experiments, built from baseline replicates a multi-arm study already owns, though not from as few as most possess.

</details>


### [174] [MidTool: Mid-training Data Synthesis for Agentic Tool Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20314)
*Fengqing Jiang,Yite Wang,Boyi Liu,Zhaoyang Wang,Canwen Xu,Zhewei Yao,Radha Poovendran,Yuxiong He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 MidTool，一个面向通用工具使用能力的中训练语料构建流水线，并验证其可显著提升大模型在多项工具使用基准上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中训练已被证明能增强数学、科学推理和软件工程智能体能力，但通用工具使用这一并行的智能体能力仍缺乏系统研究。作者希望证明通用工具使用也需要专门的中训练，而不能完全依赖后训练。

Method: 构建 MidTool 流水线，将大规模网页、PDF、代码数据与来自真实工具 API、MCP 技能和文档依据工作流的合成监督结合，形成 MidTool-Mix；用其对 Qwen3-4B-Base 和 Qwen3-8B-Base 进行中训练，随后进行监督微调和强化学习后训练，并在多个基准上比较。

Result: 相较基线，MidTool-Mix 在 SFT 和 RL 两种后训练设置下，均在 BFCL、tau2-Bench 和 MCP Universe 上持续提升下游性能。

Conclusion: 通用工具使用能力与其他关键 LLM 能力类似，能够从专门设计的中训练阶段中获益；中训练可帮助模型学习工具可用性识别、参数上下文 grounding、工具调用流程组合及不完整信息恢复。

Abstract: Mid-training is increasingly recognized as a critical stage for shaping the capabilities of large language models. Recent work has shown that targeted mid-training can strengthen reasoning-intensive abilities such as math and science, and can also improve agentic capabilities in software-engineering settings. In this work, we study the parallel but less explored agentic capability: general tool use. We present MidTool, an open corpus construction pipeline for agentic tool-use mid-training that combines large-scale web, PDF, and code data with synthesized supervision from real-world tool APIs, MCP skills, and document-grounded workflows. MidTool is designed to teach models how to recognize tool affordances, ground arguments from context, compose tool call workflow, and recover from incomplete information. We mid-train Qwen3-4B-Base and Qwen3-8B-Base on MidTool-Mix, and then apply follow-up post-training with both supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning. Compared with baselines, MidTool-Mix consistently improves downstream performance under both SFT and RL on BFCL, tau2-Bench, and MCP Universe. These results suggest that general tool use, like other important LLM capabilities, benefits from dedicated mid-training rather than being left entirely to post-training.

</details>


### [175] [Pandora's AI Model Routing Box: Efficient Allocation with Costly Value Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20316)
*Adam Fisch,Shubhendu Trivedi,Fantine Huot,William W. Cohen,Michael Kaisers,Mirella Lapata,Kate Larson,Jacob Eisenstein*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文将异构 AI 系统中的查询路由问题建模为带检查成本的 Pandora's Box 最优搜索问题，提出集中式 Pandora's Router 与去中心化 Pandora's Bidder，用价值信息准则决定是否值得进行昂贵估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构 AI 系统可通过把输入分配给最合适且成本最低的专家模型来提升质量与效率，但准确估计每个专家的预期收益本身需要成本。低成本估计器速度快但噪声大，高成本估计器更准确但代价高，因此需要系统性地权衡估计收益与估计成本。

Method: 作者将路由中的估值细化过程形式化为 Pandora's Box 问题。在高斯信号模型下推导闭式价值信息表达式，用于判断针对某个专家和输入是否应进一步调用昂贵估计器。集中式方法为 Pandora's Router；去中心化方法为 Pandora's Bidder，其中专家可独立决定是否投资自评估，再决定是否接受给定价格来处理查询。

Result: 在多 LLM 基准、检索增强专家以及具有可变推理时间设置的 LLM 三类实验中，Pandora's Router 在显著减少昂贵估计器调用次数的同时，达到接近穷举估计的路由质量。去中心化实验显示，当竞争估计较准确时，价值信息推理能提升分配效率；当估计噪声较大时，可能提高策略性专家自身效用，但损害其他参与方。

Conclusion: 论文表明，基于价值信息的估值细化策略可以有效降低异构 AI 路由中的评估成本，同时保持高质量分配；但在去中心化场景中，其效果依赖于估计准确性，并可能带来策略性行为导致的效率与公平性问题。

Abstract: Heterogeneous AI systems composed of multiple models, architectures, harnesses, or inference-time settings can improve quality and efficiency by routing queries to the specialist who can answer most effectively at the lowest cost. Routing requires estimating each specialist's expected return, but this value estimation has a cost. Cheap estimators (e.g., embedding-based predictors) are fast but noisy, while accurate estimators (e.g., fine-tuned models with access to retrieval results or partial reasoning traces) are expensive. We formalize this tradeoff as an instance of Pandora's Box, the classical problem of optimal search with costly inspection. Under a Gaussian signal model, the resulting policies have closed-form value-of-information expressions that determine, for each specialist and input, whether refining the value estimate is worth its cost. We call the centralized policy Pandora's Router. We extend this to a decentralized setting, Pandora's Bidder, where specialists independently decide whether to invest in self-assessment before accepting an offered price to claim a query. Experiments across three domains---a standard multi-LLM benchmark, retrieval-augmented specialists, and LLMs with variable inference-time reasoning---show that Pandora's Router matches the routing quality of exhaustive estimation, while querying the expensive estimator far less often. In the decentralized setting, value-of-information reasoning improves allocative efficiency when competing estimates are accurate; when competing estimates are noisy, however, it can increase the strategic specialist's utility at the expense of others.

</details>


### [176] [AI4AI-Bench: Benchmarking LLM Agents in Algorithmic Design for Recursive Self-Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20318)
*Yizhe Chi,Wenyi Li,Deyao Hong,Xiaoqiu Wang,Mingju Gao,Kaisen Yang,Bingxiang He,Youjie Zheng,Calvin Xiao,Qinhuai Na*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 AI4AI-Bench，用于衡量 AI 系统是否能改写训练算法以提升后续 AI 系统性能；当前 6 个系统 29 种配置整体表现有限，但真正修改学习过程的提交明显更有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 递归自我改进的核心在于 AI 是否能改进产生 AI 的训练算法，而现有基准多考察数据收集或超参数调优，无法隔离“设计训练算法”的能力，也难区分运行方式变化与学习机制变化。

Method: 作者构建包含 10 个冻结研究代码库的 AI4AI-Bench，覆盖 10 类训练算法。每项任务中，智能体在一块 B300 上用 4 小时改写训练算法；随后其代码从零开始重跑最多 12 小时，并由对智能体隐藏的固定评估器评分。各任务分数统一映射到同一尺度：0 表示无信息模型，0.1 表示原仓库算法，1.0 表示任务最优。

Result: 在 6 个系统的 29 种配置、全部 10 项任务上，平均分为 0.166，最佳系统为 0.250。多数提交没有真正改变模型学习方式；少数改变学习过程的提交平均得分 0.226，高于其余提交的 0.126。增加推理投入主要提高了系统愿意修改学习算法的比例，从 8% 提升到 64%，平均分从 0.094 提升到 0.196。

Conclusion: 当前 AI 系统具备一定训练算法改写能力，但距离有效支持递归自我改进仍较远。AI4AI-Bench 为持续、可复现地测量这类能力提供了基准、评估器和完整提交记录。

Abstract: Recursive self-improvement (RSI) asks whether an AI system can improve the process that produces AI systems, so that the next system inherits the improvement. That process is the training algorithm: a better objective or update rule improves the compute\mbox{-}capability exchange rate for every subsequent run, including the one that produces the next agent. Whether RSI is feasible therefore turns on whether an agent can design training algorithms. No benchmark isolates that ability: existing suites are won by collecting data or by tuning hyperparameters, and none tells a change to how a run is executed apart from a change to how the model learns. We present AI4AI\mbox{-}Bench, 10 frozen research repositories spanning 10 training algorithm families. In each task, an agent has 4 hours on one B300 to rewrite the training algorithm; its code is then rerun from scratch for up to 12 hours and scored by a fixed evaluator hidden from the agent, against the repository's original algorithm under the same procedure. Because the 10 metrics are incommensurable, every task is mapped onto one scale on which $0$ is an uninformative model, $0.1$ is the algorithm the repository ships, and $1.0$ is the task optimum. Across 29 configurations of 6 systems on all 10 tasks the mean score is $0.166$, and the best system reaches $0.250$: even the strongest closes under a fifth of the distance between the algorithm that was already there and the optimum. The submissions show where that distance went: most never change how the model learns at all, and the minority that do average $0.226$ against $0.126$ for the rest. More reasoning effort mostly buys the willingness to go there, taking that minority from $8\%$ of submissions to $64\%$ and the mean score from $0.094$ to $0.196$. We release the task suite, the evaluators and every scored submission, so that the measurement can be repeated as these systems change.

</details>


### [177] [An Agentic Approach for Active Data Collection, Travel Behavior Modeling, and Weather-Sensitive Demand Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20320)
*Narges Ahmadi,Yubo Jiao,Jônatas Augusto Manzolli,Jiangbo Yu,Luis Miranda-Moreno*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出并评估了一个三智能体工作流，将对话式出行调查、结构化数据处理与出行方式预测整合起来；在学生通勤者的天气情景选择任务中，多模态大语言模型与传统机器学习模型表现相近，最佳视觉配置达到最高准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字化数据采集与预测建模在出行行为研究中日益常见，但二者通常被分开设计和评估，缺乏一个可审计、端到端协调的流程。研究希望探索对话式调查、传统行为模型、机器学习与大语言模型预测能否在统一工作流中协同运行。

Method: 研究使用聊天机器人实施带图像增强的陈述偏好调查，收集学生通勤者在五种预设天气情景下的出行方式选择，共得到454个受访者-情景观测。通过多项Logit模型分析天气相关因素，并以逻辑回归和随机森林作为机器学习基准。同时评估9个本地部署、参数规模为2B至35B的大语言模型，在零样本提示、专家/角色设定、persona、少样本与视觉输入等配置下的预测表现。

Result: 随机森林五分类准确率为69.6%，最佳纯文本零样本大语言模型达到69.9%，无需任务特定训练。习惯出行信息带来最稳定提升，专家框架通常优于角色扮演；当缺少习惯出行信息时，persona信息更有价值。少样本提示对多个模型有提升，且少量示例后收益趋于稳定。使用与受访者相同的天气图像时，最佳视觉配置达到71.5%准确率。

Conclusion: 研究表明，对话式调查、结构化数据处理、传统行为建模、机器学习与多模态大语言模型预测可以整合进一个可审计的多智能体工作流；视觉上下文和行为习惯信息对部分模型具有额外预测价值。

Abstract: Travel behavior research increasingly combines digital data collection with predictive modeling, yet these stages are often developed and evaluated separately. This study proposes a three-agent workflow integrating conversational data collection, structured data processing, and behavioral prediction. A chatbot-administered, image-augmented stated-preference survey collected mode choices from student commuters across five predefined weather scenarios, yielding 454 respondent-scenario observations. Weather-related associations were analyzed using a multinomial logit model, while logistic regression and random forest provided machine-learning benchmarks. Nine locally deployed large language models (LLMs), ranging from 2 to 35 billion parameters, were evaluated across four zero-shot prompt-and-context conditions and extended through persona, few-shot, and vision-based configurations. Random forest achieved 69.6% five-class accuracy, while the best text-only zero-shot LLM reached 69.9% without task-specific fitting. Habitual travel information produced the most consistent gains, Expert framing generally outperformed Role-Play, and persona information was most useful when habitual travel information was unavailable. Few-shot prompting improved prediction for several models, with gains stabilizing after a small number of examples. Using the same weather images shown to respondents, the best vision-based configuration reached 71.5% five-class accuracy, indicating that visual context may provide additional predictive information for selected models. Overall, the study shows how conversational surveys, structured data processing, conventional behavioral modeling, machine learning, and multimodal LLM prediction can be coordinated within an auditable multi-agent workflow.

</details>


<div id='cs.IT'></div>

# cs.IT [[Back]](#toc)

### [178] [Assembly Theory and the Smallest Grammar Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19228)
*Wawrzyniec Bieniawski*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文评估多种压缩算法作为Assembly Index（ASI）近似估计器的有效性，并提出Re-Pair T-NDR以获得更紧的ASI上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Assembly Theory中的ASI已被证明等价于最小直线程序（SLP）的大小，这使其与数据压缩理论相关；但具体压缩算法在实践中近似ASI的效果仍缺乏系统评估。

Method: 作者在408个字符串数据集上实证比较8种压缩算法，包括基于语法和字典的方案；数据包含368个合成字符串（最大复杂度字符串及不同符号分布均衡程度的字符串）和40个自然生物序列。并提出Re-Pair T-NDR，一种带分支定界和并列规则解析的Re-Pair变体。

Result: Re-Pair T-NDR相比研究中的其他压缩算法给出了更紧的ASI上界；字母表规模增大时，压缩算法能在更长范围内贴近ASI，并推迟偏离最优组装路径的“发散点”。

Conclusion: 压缩算法可作为实际可用的ASI估计工具，其中改进的Re-Pair T-NDR在估计精度方面表现突出；符号集大小和分布特征会显著影响算法对ASI的近似能力。

Abstract: Assembly theory (AT) quantifies complexity through the assembly index (ASI) -- a metric proven equivalent to the size of the smallest straight-line program (SLP). While this equivalence links AT to data compression -- a field shaped by decades of research -- the practical efficacy of specific algorithms as ASI approximators remains largely unexplored. This study provides an empirical evaluation of eight compression algorithms, spanning grammar-based and dictionary schemes (CAs) across a dataset of 408 strings -- comprising 368 synthetic strings (including max-complexity strings and strings with varying levels of symbol distribution balance, quantified by Shannon entropy) and 40 natural biological sequences (genomic and proteomic). We introduce Re-Pair T-NDR, a branch-and-bound tie-resolving variant of Re-Pair, providing a tighter upper bound on the ASI than the other CAs researched in this study. Increasing alphabet size extends the regime in which CAs closely track the ASI, delaying a ``divergence point'', where a CA deviates from the optimal assembly path. The results establish compression algorithms as practical tools for ASI estimation.

</details>


### [179] [Tri-Hybrid Beamforming for T-RIS-Enabled Base Station](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19736)
*Hongtao Zhang,Chenlong Ding*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 论文提出面向发射端集成透射式可重构智能表面（T-RIS）的三混合MIMO建模与联合预编码框架，在数字、模拟RF与电磁孔径三域协同优化，以提升多用户下行加权和速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RIS辅助链路通常将RIS视为传播环境中的被动反射/透射节点，而发射端原生T-RIS直接参与辐射场形成，使前端电磁建模、近远场统一表征以及考虑实际硬件约束的WSR导向联合预编码变得困难。

Method: 从连续场描述出发，刻画馈源阵列、可编程T-RIS孔径与用户侧传播之间的相互作用，推导级联基带输入输出模型；并在Fresnel、Fraunhofer及混合场景下实例化同一模型。随后在功率与量化约束下，对数字预编码器、模拟网络和T-RIS系数建立WSR最大化问题，并设计两层算法：外层采用WMMSE更新，内层进行WMMSE诱导的孔径场整形与硬件投影。

Result: 仿真验证了所建模型的准确性和算法收敛性；相较基线方案，完整三混合联合设计可获得更高WSR，并能抑制混合场跨传播区域泄漏。

Conclusion: 该工作为发射端原生T-RIS三混合MU-MIMO系统提供了统一的前端建模与预编码优化方法，能够同时支持近场聚焦与远场角度波束赋形，并在实际硬件约束下提升系统频谱效率。

Abstract: Transmissive reconfigurable intelligent surfaces (T-RISs) integrated into the transmitter provide a viable realization of tri-hybrid multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO), where spatial processing is distributed across the digital, analog radio-frequency (RF), and electromagnetic (EM) domains. However, unlike conventional RIS-assisted links, a transmitter-native T-RIS directly participates in radiation formation, making T-RIS front-end modeling and weighted sum-rate (WSR)-oriented joint precoding challenging under practical hardware constraints. This paper develops a unified modeling and precoding framework for transmitter-native T-RIS tri-hybrid multi-user (MU) downlink transmission. Specifically, starting from a continuous-field description, the received field is characterized by the interaction among the feed array, the programmable T-RIS aperture, and the user-side propagation, leading to a cascaded baseband input-output model for MU precoding. Furthermore, the same representation is instantiated in the Fresnel, Fraunhofer, and mixed-field regimes, so that near-field focusing and far-field angular steering can be handled within one front-end model. Additionally, a WSR maximization problem is formulated over the digital precoder, analog network, and T-RIS coefficients under power and quantization constraints. A two-level solver is then developed by coupling outer weighted minimum mean-square error (WMMSE) updates with WMMSE-induced aperture-field shaping and hardware projection. Simulations validate the modeling accuracy and convergence, and show that the proposed full tri-hybrid design improves WSR over baselines while suppressing mixed-field cross-regime leakage.

</details>


### [180] [Resilience in Trustworthy Wireless Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19850)
*Shixiong Wang,Yumeng Zhang,Hongyu Li*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文从可信性视角系统梳理无线系统韧性，提出概念定义、层次化框架、量化维度与实现路径，并讨论韧性提升带来的多方面代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未来无线系统运行环境动态且不确定，韧性已成为关键能力；但现有研究中韧性的概念边界、支撑机制与实现技术较为分散，缺乏统一框架。

Method: 论文首先形式化定义韧性，并将其与可靠性、鲁棒性、适应性、生存性、可恢复性等相关术语区分；随后构建层次化框架，将韧性组织为能力维度、使能机制和技术量化指标；并以物理链路和无人机网络为代表场景，说明如何通过架构、运行和算法的联合设计实现韧性。

Result: 论文给出了无线系统韧性的系统性分析框架，展示了在典型无线场景中韧性可通过多层次协同设计落地，并明确了韧性工程中的关键权衡关系。

Conclusion: 提升无线系统韧性需要在资源效率、标称性能、信息获取、实现复杂度和时延之间进行折中；该框架为未来无线系统的韧性建模、评估和设计提供了结构化指导。

Abstract: Resilience has emerged as a fundamental capability for future wireless systems operating in dynamic and uncertain environments. Although resilience has attracted growing attention across academia, industry, and standardization, its conceptual scope, enabling mechanisms, and realization techniques remain fragmented. This paper presents a systematic framework of resilience in wireless systems from a trustworthiness perspective. We first formalize the concept of resilience and distinguish it from related uncertainty-aware terminologies, including reliability, robustness, adaptability, survivability, and recoverability. We then establish a hierarchical framework that organizes resilience into capability dimensions and enabling mechanisms, and that quantifies resilience through different technical aspects. We further use physical links and unmanned aerial vehicle networks as representative wireless scenarios to demonstrate how resilience can be systematically realized through the joint design of architectures, operations, and algorithms. Finally, we examine the fundamental trade-offs in resilience engineering: Improving resilience generally incurs costs in resource efficiency, nominal performance, information acquisition, implementation complexity, and latency.

</details>


### [181] [Sub-optimality of Marton's Inner Bound for the Two-Receiver Broadcast Channel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19869)
*Mian Huang,Yanxiao Liu,Yi Liu*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文证明 Marton 内界并非总能达到一般离散无记忆广播信道的容量区域，存在某些信道其容量区域严格大于 Marton 内界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Marton 内界自 1979 年提出以来一直是一般离散无记忆广播信道最著名的可达区域，但其是否等于容量区域长期未解。本文旨在解决这一核心开放问题。

Method: 作者通过构造或识别特定的离散无记忆广播信道，并对其真实容量区域与 Marton 内界进行严格比较，证明二者之间存在严格间隙。

Result: 研究表明，存在一些离散无记忆广播信道，其容量区域严格大于 Marton 内界，即 Marton 内界无法覆盖全部可达容量区域。

Conclusion: Marton 内界不是一般离散无记忆广播信道容量区域的普适精确刻画；该结果否定了其全局最优性，并提示需要新的编码策略或容量刻画方法。

Abstract: Marton's inner bound, the best-known achievable region for a general discrete memoryless broadcast channel, was proposed by Katalin Marton in 1979, and whether it always achieves the capacity region has remained open since then. In this paper, we establish its strict sub-optimality: we show that the capacity region of some discrete memoryless broadcast channels can be strictly larger than Marton's inner bound.

</details>


### [182] [A Layered Simplex Architecture for Large Alphabets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19908)
*Meir Feder,Yaniv Fogel,Ruediger Urbanke*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于大字母表概率估计的新型贝叶斯估计器：通过多个独立均匀单纯形样本逐坐标相乘并归一化构造，可对深度进行混合以避免调参；其后悔值有显式可高效计算形式，并在多类基准上接近专门方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大字母表下的概率估计在对数损失下非常重要，经典方法如 Good-Turing 已被广泛研究。但现有方法往往需要特定调参或复杂结构；作者希望构造一个简单、可解释、可计算且性能具有竞争力的贝叶斯估计器，并进一步分析其后悔值缩放规律。

Method: 提出一种乘积型贝叶斯先验/估计器：从概率单纯形中独立均匀采样多个向量，将其按坐标相乘后归一化；唯一结构参数为深度，并通过对不同深度进行混合来消除手动调参。作者推导该混合估计器相对于已知真实源编码器的后悔值显式表达式，并用于理论与实验分析。

Result: 该估计器的后悔值可显式且高效计算；在合成数据与真实文本基准上，即使没有精细调参，也能与 Good-Turing 等更专门化的方法竞争。对于 Zipf 分布且指数大于 1 的目标，在样本仅揭示小部分字母表时，后悔值近似等于已发现符号集合的描述长度加上每个符号的额外代价。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，一个结构极简的贝叶斯乘积单纯形估计器可以在大字母表概率估计中实现良好的经验性能与清晰的理论可解释性；其后悔值分析揭示了数据规模、字母表大小和深度之间的缩放规律，尤其将 Zipf 情形下的后悔增长与新符号发现率联系起来。

Abstract: Probability estimation over large alphabets under log loss is a well-studied problem, with celebrated methods such as the Good-Turing estimator. We introduce and study a new Bayesian estimator with four notable properties. First, its construction is exceptionally simple: multiply independent uniform draws from the probability simplex coordinate-wise and renormalize. Depth is the only structural parameter, and averaging over depths eliminates the need to tune it. Second, the regret of the resulting mixture, the excess code length it pays relative to a code that knows the source, admits an explicit and efficiently computable expression. Third, despite its simplicity and lack of tuned constants, the estimator is competitive across a diverse set of synthetic and real-text benchmarks with substantially more specialized methods, including Good-Turing. Fourth, the tractability of its regret allows us to identify scaling laws in data, alphabet size, and depth. For Zipf targets with exponent above one, the regret has a simple reading as long as the sample reveals only a small fraction of the alphabet. It closely matches the description length of the set of discovered symbols, at one bit of code per bit of description, plus a further cost per symbol. The data exponent is therefore the rate at which new symbols are discovered.

</details>


### [183] [Real Interference Alignment for Active IRS-Aided Systems: A Rate-Profile Learning-Based Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20007)
*Junda Liao,Quanzhong Li,Qi Zhang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出一种主动IRS辅助系统中的实干扰对齐方案，并用速率轮廓学习算法高效求解和速率最大化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动智能反射面可提供额外空间自由度，使干扰对齐以较低成本实现；但在满足用户最低速率与功率约束时，如何高效最大化系统可达和速率仍具挑战。

Method: 在直达链路阻塞且IRS仅需瞬时信道系数的假设下，设计实干扰对齐方案；提出基于速率轮廓学习的算法，利用离线训练得到的可达速率轮廓将原始优化问题解耦为多个可行性子问题，并通过广义特征值分解求解。

Result: 仿真结果显示，该算法在可达和速率性能上优于传统加权最小均方误差算法，同时显著降低程序执行时间。

Conclusion: 主动IRS结合实干扰对齐能够有效提升干扰受限系统性能；速率轮廓学习框架在性能与计算效率之间取得更优平衡，适合低复杂度资源优化场景。

Abstract: With additional spatial degrees of freedom provided by the active intelligent reflecting surface (IRS), interference alignment (IA) can be achieved at low cost. In this letter, we propose a real IA scheme for an active IRS-aided system. The proposed scheme only requires the IRS to know the instantaneous channel coefficients under the assumption of blocked direct links. To maximize the achievable sum rate subject to individual minimum rate requirements and transmission power constraints, we propose a rate-profile learning-based algorithm. The algorithm uses offline-trained achievable rate profiles to decouple the original problem into multiple feasibility subproblems, which are then solved by generalized eigenvalue decomposition. Simulation results demonstrate that our proposed algorithm outperforms the conventional weighted minimum mean square error algorithm, while requiring significantly less program execution time.

</details>


### [184] [RMWorld: Task-Aware Radio World Models with Value-of-Information Guided Multi-Trial Learning for Multi-UAV Communication Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20126)
*Xiucheng Wang,Nan Cheng,Junxi Huan*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: RMWorld 是一种面向任务的 radio world model 框架，通过把链路校准的价值信息与可信、多样的反事实试验选择耦合起来，降低多 UAV 通信控制中的决策相关信道不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多 UAV 通信控制需要在实际测量前预测空中链路服务能力；现有 radio WM 即使整体精度较高，也可能在高需求走廊或关联边界出现会改变控制决策的局部误差。现有采集与模型控制方法通常分别优化不确定性、覆盖率或乐观回报，未直接针对任务风险下降。

Method: 提出 RMWorld：用贝叶斯残差校正有偏传播公式；以“单个标签带来的、局部线性化任务积分后验速率方差的精确下降”评估链路价值；用任务门控的 log-determinant 目标选择反事实分支，并进行冲突投影与固定批量验证。

Result: 论文声称推导了方差下降恒等式，证明了后验任务风险等价性、子模贪心保证，并建立了有限范围的一阶非干扰结果。实验上，在 100 组配对 3GPP 试验中达到 0.949 bit/s/Hz 的任务加权 RMSE；在 30 组严重负载 DeepMIMO 试验中，相比 Ensemble UCB 以多 37.5% 的离线 rollout 将中位 backlog 降低 0.967。

Conclusion: RMWorld 表明，面向任务风险的信道采样与可信多样的模型 rollout 选择相结合，能比传统不确定性或乐观回报驱动方法更有效地支持多 UAV 通信控制。

Abstract: Reliable multi-UAV communication control depends on predicting which aerial links will serve traffic before measurements are available. Radio world models (radio WMs) make such planning tractable, but their errors are nonuniform: a globally accurate model may still fail along high-demand corridors or association boundaries where rate errors reverse control decisions. This mismatch creates a learning challenge. Link queries must reduce decision-relevant channel uncertainty, while counterfactual trials must be filtered so that biased rollouts do not corrupt the policy. Existing acquisition and model-based control treat these budgets separately, valuing uncertainty, coverage, or optimistic return rather than risk reduction. We present RMWorld, a task-aware radio-WM framework that couples value-of-information channel calibration with credibility-diversity multi-trial selection. A biased propagation formula is corrected by a Bayesian residual, and each link is valued by its exact one-label reduction in locally linearized task-integrated posterior rate variance. Counterfactual branches are selected by a task-gated log-determinant objective, followed by conflict projection and fixed-batch validation. We derive the variance-reduction identity, prove posterior task-risk equivalence and the submodular greedy guarantee, and establish a scoped first-order non-interference result. Across 100 paired 3GPP trials RMWorld reaches 0.949~bit/s/Hz task-weighted RMSE, and across 30 severe-load DeepMIMO trials it reduces median backlog by 0.967 versus Ensemble UCB at 37.5\% more offline rollouts.

</details>


### [185] [The Generalized Random Access Problem for Linear Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20152)
*Anina Gruica,Antonio Petrillo,Ferdinando Zullo*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 论文研究 DNA 存储中多符号随机访问的期望采样复杂度，围绕生成矩阵列采样直到指定信息符号可恢复这一模型，给出一般界、特殊编码族的闭式公式与比较基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DNA 存储需要支持随机访问，即无需测序完整编码对象即可恢复指定信息符号。已有模型主要关注单个符号恢复或全量恢复，缺少对多个指定符号同时恢复的统一极值与有限几何分析。

Method: 将单符号恢复变量 τ_i(G) 推广为集合 I 的多符号恢复变量 τ_I(G)，并定义固定请求大小 m 下的最坏情形与平均参数。基于已知的子集计数期望公式推导上下界；对系统 MDS 编码、simplex 编码及三维 balanced quasi-arcs 分别进行专门分析。

Result: 得到多符号恢复期望的一般上下界，其中下界可由单符号恢复变量的次序统计量表示，并在 m=1 时退化为已知单符号界。对系统 MDS 编码给出等价期望公式及单调性、渐近性质；对任意维 simplex 编码给出含 Gaussian binomial coefficients 的闭式公式；三维情形中比较 balanced quasi-arcs 与 simplex、MDS 基准。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了从单符号随机访问到全量覆盖深度之间的统一多符号恢复框架，揭示不同编码结构在随机访问采样复杂度上的极值特征，并为 DNA 存储编码设计提供有限几何视角下的性能基准。

Abstract: Random access is a central requirement in DNA-based storage systems: one would like to recover selected information symbols without sequencing the whole encoded object. A recent combinatorial model associates to a generator matrix $G\in F_q^{k\times n}$ the random variable $τ_i(G)$, measuring the number of sampled columns needed to recover the information vector $e_i$. We study the cardinality-based extremal and finite-geometric aspects of simultaneous multi-symbol recovery. For a nonempty set $I\subseteq[k]$, let $τ_I(G)$ denote the number of random column samples needed until all vectors $e_i$, $i\in I$, lie in the span of the observed columns. This variable interpolates between the singleton random access problem and the full-recovery problem underlying coverage depth. For each $m$, we introduce uniform worst-case and average parameters over all requested sets $I$ with $|I|=m$. Using the known subset-counting formula for $E[τ_I(G)]$, we establish general upper and lower bounds for these parameters. In particular, the lower bounds are expressed through order statistics of the singleton recovery variables and specialize to the known singleton bounds when $m=1$. For systematic MDS encoders, we record an equivalent form of the known multi-symbol expectation formula and derive monotonicity and asymptotic consequences. For simplex encoders in arbitrary dimension, we obtain closed formulae in terms of Gaussian binomial coefficients; the full-recovery endpoint agrees with the known coverage-depth formula for simplex codes. Finally, in dimension three we study balanced quasi-arcs and compare their values with the simplex and MDS benchmarks.

</details>


### [186] [Algorithms, Complexity, and Entropy of the Bernard-Letac Fair-Sampling Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20234)
*Claude Gravel*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文扩展并细化了 Bernard–Letac 从未知偏置独立同分布源中进行 m 个结果均匀随机采样的方法，给出算法、正确性与复杂度分析，并对素数情形和二元情形提供更精确的期望成本结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典 Bernard–Letac 构造能够从未知偏置源中生成均匀随机结果，但其计算实现、停止时间、信息论代价以及特殊参数情形下的精确性质仍缺乏系统分析。本文旨在补足这些理论与算法层面的缺口。

Method: 论文提出五个具有形式化正确性保证的算法，并进行复杂度分析；在 m 为素数 p 时，利用源分布的 Rényi 熵推导期望抽样次数的精确乘积公式；同时研究一阶近似、熵下界与 p 趋近 1 的极限行为；对二元游走构造七状态自动机来计算 mod-2 first-passage kernel，以改进公平分配成本。

Result: 得到素数情形下期望抽样次数的精确公式；证明一阶近似会一致高估真实期望，且熵下界无法达到；当 p 趋近 1 时，期望成本收敛到一个大于 1 且由完整源分布决定的常数；七状态自动机将二元情形的公平分配成本由二次复杂度降低到近线性复杂度。

Conclusion: 该研究显著推进了 Bernard–Letac 随机抽样框架的算法化与信息论理解，尤其在素数输出规模和二元源场景中给出了更精确的成本刻画与更高效的计算方案。

Abstract: Bernard and Letac (1971) introduced a method for uniform random sampling among m outcomes from an unknown biased source of independent and identically distributed symbols. The process terminates when the multinomial coefficient of the cumulative symbol counts equals zero modulo m. This study extends the computational and information-theoretic analysis of their construction by presenting five algorithms with formal correctness guarantees and comprehensive complexity analyses. For prime m = p, the Bernard-Letac framework is analyzed in greater detail. The Rényi entropies of the source yield an exact product formula for the expected number of draws. A first-order approximation consistently overestimates this value, and the entropy lower bound is never attained. As p approaches 1, the expected cost converges to a constant greater than 1, determined by the entire source distribution. Furthermore, a seven-state automaton computes the mod-2 first-passage kernel of the binary walk, reducing the fair assignment cost from quadratic to nearly linear.

</details>


### [187] [The Honeycomb Framework for Code Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20287)
*William Gay,Fernando Granha Jeronimo,Lenny Liu*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 论文提出 honeycomb hierarchy 表示论框架，用于改进二元码率函数 R_2(δ) 的渐近上界，并在若干区间优于既有组合界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有二元码渐近上界（如 whole-cube 分支、constant-weight 分支及 MRRW 相关界）仍存在可改进空间。作者希望通过更精细的表示论结构与层级化优化，统一并强化既有上界。

Method: 构造 honeycomb hierarchy：第一层基于两行超八面体表示图和坐标 box-transfer channel，结合 moving-projection theorem 得到四参数指数 κ_HC；进一步提出表示深度、anchor 深度以及 Horn–channel hierarchy 的矩阵优化推广。

Result: 证明 κ_best=min{κ_CW,κ_HC} 不劣于既有 κ_bin=min{κ_CW,κ_H}，且低于若干先前一般上界；κ_H 是已有 whole-cube/MQC 界的特例，M_2 对应一个精确对称切片。低层 Horn–channel 优化已可增强此前最强一般界。

Conclusion: honeycomb hierarchy 为二元码率函数上界提供了更强且可扩展的表示论优化框架，并通过增加层级深度有望逐步逼近精确量 A_2(n,d)，为进一步收紧编码理论上界开辟路径。

Abstract: We introduce the honeycomb hierarchy, a representation-theoretic framework that gives new asymptotic upper bounds on $R_2(δ)$. Its first level is the two-row hyperoctahedral representation graph associated with type $S^{(n-k,k)}$. Retaining every two-row irreducible and every coordinate box-transfer channel, together with a moving-projection theorem, yields an explicit four-parameter exponent $κ_{\mathrm{HC}}$. The earlier whole-cube exponent $κ_H$ is a boundary restriction of this optimization, whereas the fully optimized second MRRW exponent $M_2$ is an exact symmetric slice.
  The prior best curve is the combined $κ_{\mathrm{bin}}=\min\{κ_{\mathrm{CW}},κ_H\}$, which uses a constant-weight branch $κ_{\mathrm{CW}}$. Replacing only the whole-cube branch by the honeycomb bound gives $κ_{\mathrm{best}}=\min\{κ_{\mathrm{CW}}, κ_{\mathrm{HC}}\}$. We prove, on $0<δ<1/2$, \[
  R_2(δ)\le κ_{\mathrm{best}}(δ)
  \le κ_{\mathrm{bin}}(δ)
  \le R_{\mathrm{2MQC}}(δ)<M_2(δ),\\[-1mm]
  κ_{\mathrm{best}}(δ)
  \le \min\{κ_{\mathrm{CW}}(δ),
  κ_{\mathrm{bal}}(δ)\}
  <R_{\mathrm{2MQC}}(δ),
  \qquad
  κ_H(δ)=R_{\mathrm{MQC}}(δ). \]
  The hierarchy has two further directions. Increasing the representation depth replaces scalar by matrix-valued transfers on the hive. Increasing the anchor depth localizes it in a stable-set hierarchy. The resulting bounds are monotone in both directions and eventually recover $A_2(n,d)$. A complementary Horn--channel hierarchy gives matrix optimizations whose $2\times2$ level is $κ_{\mathrm{HC}}$ and whose $3\times3$ level is a stronger bound. Already at low levels, they can be used to improve the strongest previous general bounds, while the honeycomb framework provides a route towards tighter bounds.

</details>
